Logit Adam details:
```
def logit_lse_step(x, beta):
    a = softmax(x)
    score, ga = lse_score_grad_a(a, beta)
    # center a? assume sum=1
    gx = a * (ga - np.dot(a, ga))
    return score, gx, a
```

Implementation of lse_score_grad_norm:
```
def lse_score_grad_norm(a, beta):
    n=a.shape[0]
    c=fast_conv(a)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-12: return inf zeros
    cmax=float(c.max())
    scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
    # for numerical, if beta huge and c-cmax underflows okay
    exps=np.exp(scaled)
    exp_sum=exps.sum()
    if exp_sum<=0: p=np.zeros_like(c); p[int(c.argmax())]=1
    else: p=exps/exp_sum
    L=cmax + np.log(exp_sum)/beta
    score=2*n*L/(S*S)
    # grad
    ra=a[::-1]
    M2=3*n-2
    nf2=_nfft(M2)
    RA=np.fft.rfft(ra, nf2)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p, nf2)
    convres=np.fft.irfft(RA*P, nf2)[:2*n-1] # length 3n-2? Wait RA length n, P length 2n-1 => conv length n+2n-1-1=3n-2. slice n-1:2n-1 length n.
    g=2.0*convres[n-1:2*n-1]
    grad=(2.0*n)*(g/(S*S) - 2.0*L/(S**3))
    return score, grad
```
Need for n=1 handle M2=1? nf2 next power of 0? `_nfft(1)=1`? bit_length of 0 is 0, 1<<0=1. okay.

But exps for c near cmax all ~1 if beta small; if beta large and many small underflow, exp_sum at least one 1. okay.

Memory: p length 2n-1, exps same; okay.

For exact subgradient, implement:
```
def exact_score_grad_a(a):
    c=fast_conv(a); S=sum; M=c.max; p = uniform over active where c>=M-1e-12? or top K. score=2nM/S². compute convres similar with p.
```
Use for line search maybe. Could top K average: p = zeros; idx = np.argsort(-c)[:K]; p[idx]=1/K. This smooths subgrad. Maybe beneficial in logit Adam high beta? But LSE already.

Logit functions:
```
def stable_softmax(x):
    x=x - x.max()
    z=np.exp(x)
    s=z.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.full(len(x),1/len)
    return z/s
```
But if x mean huge and max subtract, exp of negative large underflows to 0. Good. For gradient, x can be large but centered.
```
def logit_grad(x, ga, a):
    return a*(ga - np.dot(a, ga))
```
Because sum ga? If ga not constant, dot.
```
def logit_lse_score_grad(x, beta):
    a=stable_softmax(x)
    sc, ga = lse_score_grad_norm(a,beta)
    gx = a*(ga - np.dot(a,ga))
    return sc,gx,a
```
But lse_score_grad_norm computes FFT. Could we avoid separate gradient in logit by using p? yes.

Parent fit function:
```
def fit_fourier_x(a, M):
   n=len(a); z=np.log(np.clip(a,1e-30,1e30) / (a.sum()/n))
   z -= z.mean()
   zr=np.fft.rfft(z)
   x=[]
   for k in 1..M:
      xr = 2*zr[k].real/n; xr = -2*zr[k].imag/n; x += [xr,xr]
   return np.array(x)
```

i = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
F=[]
for m in range(1,M+1):

F=np.vstack(F)

```
def optimize_fourier(n, M, x0, time_budget, t_start, beta_steps, maxiter_per):
    F = build_features(n,M)
    bounds=[(-3,3)]*(2*M)
    x=x0 clipped
    best_score=inf; best_a=None
    # beta schedule maybe [30,120,600,3000,12000]
    # allocate time fractions, each run:
    for beta:
       set PG F,beta
       res = minimize(pg_func,x,jac=pg_grad,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-6,'maxls':40})
       x=clip(res.x)
       a=make_a(x); exact update
       if time left break
    return best_score,best_a,x
```

```
def active_subgrad_polish(a, time_budget, t_start, topk=10):
    n=len(a); x=np.log(np.clip(a,1e-12,1e12)); x-=x.mean()
    best_a=a; best_score=compute_score(a)
    # momentum? Use simple step with topK
    for step in range(...):
       a=stable_softmax(x)
       c=fast_conv(a); score=2n*c.max
       idx=np.argpartition(-c, topk)[:topk]
       p=np.zeros(len(c)); p[idx]=1/topk
       L=float(p@c)
       # grad_a for weighted score 2n Σp c /S² ~ 2n*(2 conv -2L)
       ga = weighted_grad_a(c,p,L,n)  # use convres as above with p
       gx = a*(ga - np.dot(a,ga))
       # line search
       accepted=False
       for eta in [0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
          x_try = x - eta*gx
          x_try -= x_try.mean(); x_try=np.clip(x_try,-40,40)
          a_try=stable_softmax(x_try)
          sc_try=compute_score(a_try)
          if sc_try < best_score - 1e-12:
             x=x_try; a=a_try; best_score=sc_try; best_a=a_try.copy(); accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
          # maybe random small step? break or reduce topk
       if time left break
    return best_score,best_a
```
But `compute_score` each trial uses FFT. topk maybe 20. Could be useful. If no improvement, we can shuffle topk or use random active peaks. But LSE high already.

def weighted_grad_a(p, a, L, n):
   ra=a[::-1]; convres=...; g=2*convres[n-1:2n-1]
   grad=2n*(g - 2L)  # if S=1
```
This assumes S=1. Good. For topK p sum=1. The objective 2n Σp c; not max, but if top are near max, direction helps.

def make_harmonic_inits(n, rng, count=12):
   inits=[]
   for trial:
      a=np.ones(n)
      # add up to 2 harmonics
      for m in [1,2,3]:
         amp = rng.uniform(0.1,0.9) if m==1 else rng.uniform(0.05,0.4)
         ph = rng.uniform(0,2π)
         a += amp*np.cos(2π*m*i/n+ph)
      a=np.maximum(a,1e-4); norm
   # specific phase: for r in [0.3,0.6,0.9], a=1+r*np.sin(2π*i/n+π/2)
   return inits
```

`fast_conv(a)`:
```
n=a.size
if n==0: return np.array([])
if n<64: return np.convolve(a,a)
M=2*n-1
N=_next_power_of_two(M)
A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
return np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:M]
```
For small np.convolve exact faster. For n=1, M=1, N=1.

c=fast_conv(a)
M=float(np.max(c))
return float(2.0*n*M/(S*S))
```
No normalization; scale invariant. If a contains nan? return inf? Add check maybe.

def logit_lse_score_grad(x,beta):

   sc, ga = lse_score_grad_arr(a,beta)

def active_polish(a_init,time_budget,t_start):
   n=len(a_init)
   x=np.log(np.clip(a_init,1e-12,1e12)); x-=x.mean()
   a=stable_softmax(x)
   best=compute_score(a); best_a=a.copy()
   topk = min(20, max(5, n//200))
   step=0
   while time.monotonic() < t_start+time_budget:
       c=fast_conv(a); M=float(c.max())
       # if M - median >? This is score/2n.
       idx=np.argpartition(-c, topk)[:topk]
       p=np.zeros(len(c)); p[idx]=1.0/topk
       L=float(np.dot(p,c))
       ga=weighted_grad_a(a,p,L,n)
       gx=a*(ga-np.dot(a,ga))
       # line search
       best_step=None
       for eta in [0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.005]:
          x2=x-eta*gx; x2-=x2.mean(); x2=np.clip(x2,-60,60); a2=stable_softmax(x2); sc2=compute_score(a2)
          if sc2 < best - 1e-12:
             best_step=(x2,a2,sc2); break
       if best_step is None:
          # try smaller random active weights? shift idx shotgun
          idx2 = (idx + n//3) % (2*n-1)? Not meaningful. Could break.
          # occasional random small x step
          if step % 5 ==4:
             x2=x+0.05*np.random.default_rng(step).standard_normal(n); ...
          else: break
       if best_step:
          x,a,best=best_step; best_a=a.copy()
       step+=1
   return best,best_a
```

def make_sinusoid_inits(n,rng,count=8):
    i=np.arange(n,float)
    out=[]
    # uniform maybe not
    for r in (0.3,0.6,0.9):
       for ph in (np.pi/2, 0.0, rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)):
          a=1.0 + r*np.cos(2*np.pi*i/n + ph)
          a=np.maximum(a,1e-6); a/=a.sum(); out.append(a)
    # two harmonics random
    for _ in range(max(0,count-9)):
       a=np.ones(n)
       for m in (1,2,3):
          amp = rng.uniform(0.1,0.5) / m
          amp2 = rng.uniform(0.05,0.3) / m
          ph = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi); ph2 = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
          a += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*i/n+ph) + amp2*np.sin(...)
       a=np.maximum(a,1e-6); a/=a.sum(); out.append(a)
    return out

def pg_compute(x):
    x = np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    if PG['cache_x'] is not None:
       if x.shape == PG['cache_x'].shape and np.array_equal(x,PG['cache_x']):
           return PG['cache_score'], PG['cache_gx']
    F=PG['F']; beta=PG['beta']
    z=x.dot(F)
    a=soft_from_z(z)
    sc, ga = lse_score_grad_arr(a,beta)
    gx = a*(ga - np.dot(a,ga))
    PG['cache_x']=x.copy(); PG['cache_score']=sc; PG['cache_gx']=gx
    return sc,gx

def softmax_from_z(z):

    s=e.sum()

       a=np.full(z.shape,1.0/z.size); a[np.argmax(z)]=?

def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
       seq=height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
       return None
    try:
       p=np.asarray(seq,dtype=float).ravel()
    except Exception:
       return None
    if p.size==0: return None
    p=np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(p.sum())
    if s<1e-12: return None
    return p/s
```

```
def feature_scale(m):
    if m <= 2: return 1.0
    return 1.0 / math.sqrt(float(m))
```

`build_feature_matrix(n,M)`:
```
def build_feature_matrix(n,M):
   i=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
   if M<=0: return np.zeros((0,n))
   rows=[]
   for m in range(1,M+1):
       w=2.0*math.pi*float(m)/float(n)
       s=feature_scale(m)
       rows.append(np.cos(w*i)*s)
       rows.append(np.sin(w*i)*s)
   return np.vstack(rows).astype(np.float64)
```
For n=1, w=2πm, sin zero, cos1. Fine.

`fit_fourier_x(a,M)`:
```
def fit_fourier_x(a,M):
   n=a.size
   if M<=0: return np.zeros(0)
   z=np.log(np.clip(a,1e-30,1e30).astype(float))
   z -= float(np.mean(z))  # mean log not exactly log mean but constant irrelevant. To fit z relative to log(mean) maybe subtract log(sum/n). Use mean easier; constant irrelevant? But if z mean not zero, the DFT DC omitted. The low coefficients unaffected by subtracting any constant. So z-=mean.
   Z=np.fft.rfft(z)
   x=np.empty(2*M)
   for m in range(1,M+1):
      s=feature_scale(m)
      c=2.0*float(Z[m].real)/float(n)
      sn=-2.0*float(Z[m].imag)/float(n)
      x[2*(m-1)] = c/s
      x[2*(m-1)+1] = sn/s
   return x
```
If m >= len(Z), M may exceed n/2? We'll cap M <= n//2 - maybe. In calls, M=min(160,n//4). For n small. In function, limit `M=min(M,max(0,n//2))`.
```
M = min(M, max(0, n//2))

def make_sinusoid_inits(n, rng, count=8):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    out=[]
    if n <= 0: return out
    phases = [math.pi/2, 0.0, math.pi, rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*math.pi)]
    for r in (0.25,0.5,0.75,0.95):
       for ph in phases[:3]:
          a = 1.0 + r * np.cos(2.0*math.pi*i/n + ph)
          a = np.maximum(a, 1e-6)
          s = a.sum()
          if s > 0: out.append(a/s)
    # already 12; count random
    extra = max(0, count - len(out))
    for _ in range(extra):
       a = np.ones(n)
       for m in (1,2,3):
          amp = rng.uniform(0.1,0.6)/float(m)
          ph = rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*math.pi)
          a += amp*np.cos(2.0*math.pi*m*i/n + ph)
          amp2 = rng.uniform(0.05,0.25)/float(m)
          ph2 = rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*math.pi)
          a += amp2*np.sin(2.0*math.pi*m*i/n + ph2)
       a = np.maximum(a,1e-6); s=a.sum(); if s>0: out.append(a/s)
    # If want fewer, return first count? We may not need limit. Return out.
    return out
```
This makes 12+ extra. Evaluate all maybe. Could be 20, fine.

def run_param_optimization(n,M,x0,time_budget,t_start,F=None):
    if time_budget <=0 or M<=0 or F is None and ...:
       ...
    if F is None: F=build_feature_matrix(n,M)
    V=F.shape[0]
    if V==0:
       a=np.ones(n)/n; return compute_score(a),a
    x=np.array(x0,dtype=float).copy()
    if x.size != V:
       # pad or truncate
       if x.size < V: x = np.concatenate([x, np.zeros(V-x.size)])
       else: x=x[:V]
    x = np.clip(x, -3.0, 3.0)
    beta_schedule=[20.0,80.0,400.0,2000.0,10000.0]
    # if time_budget small adjust
    n_beta=len(beta_schedule)
    best_score=np.inf; best_a=None
    x=np.clip(x,-3,3)
    for idx,beta in enumerate(beta_schedule):
       # beta deadline
       if idx == n_beta-1:
          frac=0.3 + 0.7/n_beta  # larger final? For 5:0.44
       else:
          frac=0.07 + 0.13/(n_beta?)? Simpler: equal 0.2.
```

```
fracs=[]
for i in range(n_beta):

def finalize_output(best_a, parent, t_deadline):
    candidates=[]
    if best_a is not None: candidates.append(best_a)
    if parent is not None and (best_a is None or len(parent)!=len(best_a)):
        candidates.append(parent)
    best_out=None; best_val=np.inf
    verify_limit = 15000
    for a in candidates:
       if a is None: continue
       if len(a) > verify_limit:
          # don't call evaluator, rely internal? But need choose. Use compute_score.
          val = compute_score(a)
       else:
          try: val = float(evaluate_sequence([float(max(0.0,min(1000.0,x))) for x in a]))
          except Exception: val=compute_score(a)
       if val < best_val:
          best_val=val; best_out=a
    if best_out is None: best_out=np.ones(64)
    out=[float(max(0.0,min(1000.0,x))) for x in best_out]
    s=float(sum(out))
    if s < 0.01: out=[1.0/64]*64
    # normalize? Score scale invariant; but if evaluator clips, no need. Could normalize to sum 1 to be safe. If we normalize out, evaluator same. Do out = [x/ (s/ len?)? Actually scale invariant any scale; but values within [0,1000]. Sum 1 fine.
    # If we rescale, no harm. But sum already ~1. If parent raw sum maybe not 1? We normalized parent. best a from softmax sum1.
    return out
```

In `active_polish`, if `n` small topk. Implementation:
```
def active_polish(a_init,time_budget,t_start):
    if time_budget <=0 or a_init is None: return compute_score(a_init),a_init
    a=np.asarray(a_init,float); n=len(a)
    x=np.log(np.clip(a,1e-12,1e12)); x-=x.mean()
    a=stable_softmax(x)
    best=compute_score(a); best_a=a.copy()
    topk=max(1,min(3*n//2, n))
    deadline=t_start+time_budget
    step=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c=fast_conv(a)
       C=float(c.max())
       if not finite: break
       idx=np.argpartition(-c, topk)[:topk]
       p=np.zeros(len(c)); p[idx]=1.0/float(topk)
       L=float(np.dot(p,c))
       ga=weighted_grad_a(a,p,L,n)
       gx=a*(ga - np.dot(a,ga))
       nrm=np.linalg.norm(gx)
       if nrm <= 1e-14: break
       dirx=-gx/nrm
       improved=False
       for eps in (0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005):
           x2=x+eps*dirx
           x2-=x2.mean(); x2=np.clip(x2,-60,60)
           a2=stable_softmax(x2)
           sc2=compute_score(a2)
           if sc2 < best - 1e-12:
               x=x2; a=a2; best=sc2; best_a=a2.copy(); improved=True; break
       if not improved:
           # try decreasing (positive) direction maybe? no
           step +=1
           if step > 5: break
    return best,best_a
```
It moves along negative normalized gradient. Since topk weights, L=better? Good.

`weighted_grad_a` formula for S=1: `grad = 2n*(g - 2L)`. Let's verify with p sum1. If p active only lag k*, derivative of 2n c_k =2n*2 a_{k-i}=4n a_{k-i}. Our convolution with rev and p (sum1 at k*) gives convres = a_{k*-i}? g=2*...; grad=2n*(2a - 2*1?) Wait L=c_k = Σ a_i a_{k-i}. grad should 2n * 2 a_{k-i} =4n a_{k-i} (since sum constraint? We are optimizing on softmax, no sum constraint separate? In logit, sum constraint enforced; the gradient wrt x has no Lagrange. If we use weighted objective with softmax, gradient wrt a for unconstrained a should include no -? For softmax parametrization, we use gradient wrt a of objective with no constraint (since constraint built). Weighted objective 2n Σ p c. ∂/∂a = 4n * contributions. Our formula `g=2*convres` where convres is a_{k-i}. grad=2n*g=4n a_{k-i}. Good. Where did `-2L` in LSE? For LSE score =2n L(a), L already quotient? For normalized sum=1, L=... But derivative of L with respect to a includes -2L/S term? Wait L = cmax + logsumexp(β(c-cmax))/β, no division by S? In `lse_score_grad_arr`, score=2n L/S². In implicit calculus, because a(x) sum=1, but if we consider F(a) defined only on simplex, the gradient in ambient used for chain rule should be the projection of the derivative? Standard: If a=softmax(x), ∂F/∂x = J^T grad_a where grad_a can be any vector representing gradient of F with respect to a on the simplex? If F(a) is defined for all a with sum normalization included (score = 2n max c / S²), we can take unconstrained gradient. If we instead define G(a)=2n max c(a) on the simplex, the gradient on tangent space is projection of that. The softmax gradient formula with gx = a*(ga - a·ga) works for ga being gradient of a function defined as 2n c(a) (not divided by S²) if no normalization penalty? Let's derive.

Softmax a(x). F(x)=C(a(x)) where C(a)=2n max c(a) (assuming we want normalized sum but a softmax has sum1). The differential dC = Σ ga_i da_i, where ga_i = ∂C/∂a_i? If C only depends on ratios? C is homogeneous degree 2 in a, but on sum1 not. The gradient on ambient of C is fine. The chain rule with Σ ga_i da_i uses gauge? For variations da from softmax, sum da=0. If ga has a constant component, it doesn't affect tangent. The formula gx = a*(ga - mean ga*a?)? Actually for softmax, ∂a_i/∂x_k = a_i(f_ik) - a_i a_j? In coordinate logit with x_j direct features f_i? For full coordinate x not features, a=softmax(x), J_{ik}=a_i(δ_ik - a_k). Then ∂F/∂x_k = Σ_i ga_i a_i(δ_ik - a_k) = a_k[g_k - Σ_j a_j g_j]. Yes this formula uses ambient gradient ga. It automatically drops constant components. Good.

For C(a)=2n max c, ∂C/∂a_i = 4n a_{k-i} for active (plus if subgradient). No -term. For LSE surrogate used with unconstrained a in `lse_score_grad_arr`, score=2n L/S²; gradient includes - term because S varies. But when embedding in softmax, we should use gradient of score as a function of a with S? Since score is scale invariant, defining score(a)=2n L(a)/S² for all a. The derivative includes -term, but on the simplex, is that equivalent to gradient of quotient restricted? Let's check. If we use `lse_score_grad_arr` at a sum=1, grad =2n*(g/S² - 2L/S³) =2n*(g -2L) (S=1). This includes -2L. If we instead use C(a)=2n L(a) (without S²) gradient =2n*g. Which is correct for softmax? Since scale is fixed by softmax, the scale-invariant objective quotient evaluated at a sum1. If we differentiate the quotient as a function on R_+ and then chain through softmax, the -term arises from derivative of 1/S². But for parameter x, a(x) is normalized; the scale variation along x is already captured by softmax Jacobian. The chain rule with ambient gradient of the quotient (including -2L) plus softmax Jacobian: gx = a*(g -2L - mean(a*(g-2L))). The constant -2L part drops (since mean term removes constant? Let's compute: add constant c to ga_i: ga_i'=ga_i+c. Then a*(ga_i' - a·ga') = a*(ga_i+c - (a·ga + c∑a)) = a*(ga_i - a·ga) because ∑a=1. So constant -2L drops! Thus using full quotient gradient or C gradient differ by a constant vector -2L, which disappears in softmax gradient. Excellent. Therefore `gx = a*(ga - dot(a,ga))` with ga from `lse_score_grad_arr` is same as using 2n*g with C gradient (constant removed). Good. For weighted topk, using ga=4n contributions (no -L) also fine; but if we used LSE formula with -L, dot term removes constant. Important: In parameter optimization, we also use `lse_score_grad_arr` with S maybe 1 and then gx formula, constant drops. Fine.

Now, `weighted_grad_a` should use general S? a sum1. okay.

Let's verify `lse_score_grad_arr` gradient sign for minimization: score=2n L/S², grad = 2n/S² ∂L - 4nL/S³. In code g=2*convres (∂L? Wait L = cmax + logsumexp/β. ∂L/∂a_j = (1/β) Σ exp(...)*∂c_k = Σ p_k ∂c_k = g? We set g=2*convres; yes ∂L/∂a_j = g_j. Then grad = 2n*(g/S² - 2L/S³). Good.

Constant drop: If we used C=2nL, gradient=2n*g, gx = a*(2n*g - mean) = 2n a*(g-mean(g*a?) Wait dot(a,2n*g)=2n a·g; with quotient grad: 2n(g-2L), dot=2n a·g -2n*2L; gx quotient=2n a*(g-2L) -2n(a·g -2L)a =2n a*g -2n*2La -2n a·g a +2n*2La a =2n a*(g - a? No: a*(g) - (a·g)a. Exactly same as C gradient. Good.

Now, in `pg_compute`, we compute score with S maybe not exactly 1. But `ga` uses S. For softmax, S=1; but if numerical S slightly off, constant drop approximate. Fine. We can normalize a before calling lse to sum 1 to reduce. In `pg_make_a`, returns sum1. In logit `stable_softmax` sum1. Good.

Maybe in lse_score_grad_arr, if S not1, constant term doesn't exactly drop due S? But close.

Now, feature gradient: We used grad_x = F.T@h - (h@a) mean? For general feature matrix with V, x parameterizes z = F x (rows features). The softmax gradient from x to z: z depends linearly F x. Then dx_k = Σ_i a_i? Need derive carefully. Let F shape V×n? We stored rows per variable: z_i = Σ_m x_m F_{m,i}. In pg_make_a: `z = x.dot(F)`, F (V,n). For softmax wrt z, ∂a_i/∂z_j = a_i(δ_ij - a_j). Then ∂a_i/∂x_m = Σ_j ∂a_i/∂z_j ∂z_j/∂x_m = Σ_j a_i(δ_ij - a_j) F_{m,j} = a_i(F_{m,i} - Σ_j a_j F_{m,j}). Let h_i = ga_i a_i. Gradient wrt x_m = Σ_i h_i F_{m,i} - Σ_i h_i Σ_j a_j F_{m,j} = dot(h,F_row) - (dot(h,a)) dot(a,F_row). We compute `h = ga*a`; `dot` = F.T @ h; `mean_a` = F.T @ a; scalar = h.dot(a); gx = dot - scalar*mean_a. Good.

If F has no constant feature, there is no null constant. Good.

Now, feature scale: In `build_feature_matrix`, if M=0 returns (0,n). `pg_make_a` with V=0? In run_param handle.

Edge: In `fit_fourier_x`, if a has zeros, log clip; for M > n//2, indices beyond n/2: rfft length n//2+1. If M too high, would error. We set M <= min(160,n//4). In `param_global_search`, ensure `M = max(1, min(M, n//4))`. For n=100, M=25. okay. `fit` called with that M.

Potential performance: `make_sinusoid_inits` uses `math.pi*m*i/n` where i array and m int; computes for each m; okay. For n=16384, 12 seq, compute score each via FFT. okay.

The initial beta low and high may cause x to drift, but if objective hits poor high amplitude, LSE may prefer sparse near-zero, score high. But multiple restarts. Full logit from parent may be safer. We can also pass local_best parent to each run; if random run bad, best unchanged.

```
def _next_power_of_two(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```
For m=0? returns1.

def compute_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size ==0: return np.inf
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S <=0 or not np.isfinite(S): return np.inf
    c=fast_conv(a)
    if c.size==0 or not np.isfinite(c).all(): return np.inf? Checking all finite maybe O(n), okay. Use `M=float(np.max(c))`; if not finite return inf.
    return float(2.0*a.size*M/(S*S))
```

`lse_score_grad_arr`:
```
def lse_score_grad_arr(a,beta):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    S=float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-300: return np.inf, np.zeros(n)
    c=fast_conv(a)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax): return np.inf, np.zeros(n)
    beta=max(1.0,float(beta))
    scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
    exps=np.exp(scaled)
    exp_sum=float(np.sum(exps))
    if exp_sum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(exp_sum):
       p=np.zeros_like(c); idx=int(np.argmax(c)); p[idx]=1.0; L=cmax
    else:
       p=exps/exp_sum
       L=cmax + float(np.log(exp_sum))/beta
    ra=a[::-1].copy()
    M2=3*n-2
    N2=_next_power_of_two(M2)
    RA=np.fft.rfft(ra,N2)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,N2)
    convres=np.fft.irfft(RA*P,N2)[:M2]
    g=2.0*convres[n-1:2*n-1]
    S2=S*S; S3=S2*S
    grad=2.0*n*(g/S2 - 2.0*L/S3)
    return float(2.0*n*L/S2), grad
```
If n=1, M2=1, convres len1, slice 0:1 ok. `ra=a[::-1].copy()` length1.

`softmax_from_z`:
```
def softmax_from_z(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64)
    z=np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    zmax=float(np.max(z))
    z=z-zmax
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=float(np.sum(e))
    if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a=np.full(z.size,1.0/float(z.size))
        if z.size>0:
           k=int(np.argmax(z)); a[k]=? not needed.
        return a
    return e/s
```
If z.size 0? not. If all e underflow? zmax finite, exp(max)=1, sum>=1. Unless z all nan? replaced 0. good.

`stable_softmax` can call.

`pg_compute`:
```
def pg_compute(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    if PG['cache_x'] is not None and x.shape == PG['cache_x'].shape:
        if np.array_equal(x, PG['cache_x']):
            return PG['cache_score'], PG['cache_gx']
    F=PG['F']; beta=PG['beta']
    if F is None:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(x.size)
    z=x.dot(F)
    a=softmax_from_z(z)
    sc, ga = lse_score_grad_arr(a,beta)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        ga=np.zeros_like(a)
    gx=a*(ga - float(np.dot(a,ga)))
    gx = np.where(np.isfinite(gx), gx, 0.0)
    PG['cache_x']=x.copy()
    PG['cache_score']=sc
    PG['cache_gx']=gx
    return sc,gx
```
If x contains nan, z nan-> softmax replaced 0, sc maybe 2, gradient zero? But minimize won't like. Bounds prevent nan.

def run_param_optimization(n,M,x0,time_budget,t_start,F=None):
    if time_budget <=0 or n<=0:
       return None,None
    if F is None:
        F=build_feature_matrix(n,M)
    V=F.shape[0]
    if V==0:
       a=np.ones(n)/float(n); return compute_score(a),a
    x=np.asarray(x0,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if x.size < V:
        pad=np.zeros(V-x.size); x=np.concatenate([x,pad])
    elif x.size > V:
        x=x[:V]
    x=np.clip(x,-3.0,3.0)
    beta_schedule=[20.0,100.0,600.0,3000.0,12000.0]
    n_beta=len(beta_schedule)
    if n_beta<=1: fracs=[1.0]
    else:
       final=0.4; base=(1.0-final)/float(n_beta-1)
       fracs=[base]*(n_beta-1)+[final]
    bounds=[(-3.0,3.0)]*V
    best_score=np.inf; best_a=None
    cum=0.0
    for idx,beta in enumerate(beta_schedule):
        now=time.monotonic()
        if now >= t_start+time_budget: break
        cum += fracs[idx]
        beta_deadline = t_start + time_budget * min(1.0, cum)
        time_per_beta = max(0.01, beta_deadline - now)
        pg_set(F,beta)
        try:
            t_est=time.monotonic()
            sc0,gx0=pg_compute(x)
            per=max(1e-4, time.monotonic()-t_est)
            maxiter=int(np.clip(0.8*time_per_beta/per, 15, 300))
        except Exception:
            maxiter=50
        try:
            res=minimize(pg_func, x, jac=pg_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
                         options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-11, 'gtol':1e-5, 'maxls':50, 'disp':False})
            xf=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
            if xf.size==V and np.all(np.isfinite(xf)):
               x=np.clip(xf,-3.0,3.0)
        except Exception as e:
            pass
        a=pg_make_a(x)
        sc=compute_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score=sc; best_a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0).copy()
    if best_a is None:
        a=pg_make_a(x); best_score=compute_score(a); best_a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0).copy()
    return best_score,best_a
```

def param_global_search(parent,best_score,best_a,t_start,deadline,seed=999):
    if deadline <= t_start:
       return best_score,best_a
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    order=[]
    if parent is not None:
       p_n=parent.size
       if 256 <= p_n <= 30000:
          order.append(p_n)
    for n in (8192,16384,4096):
       if n not in order:
          order.append(n)
       if len(order) >= 4: break
    weights=[]
    total=deadline-t_start
    if total <=0: return ...
    for idx,n in enumerate(order):
       if idx==0: weights.append(0.40)
       elif idx==1: weights.append(0.25)
       else: weights.append(0.15)
    wsum=float(sum(weights)); weights=[w/wsum for w in weights]
    cum=0.0
    for idx,n in enumerate(order):
       cum += weights[idx]
       n_deadline = min(deadline, t_start + total*min(1.0,cum))
       if n < 256: continue
       M = max(20, min(160, n//50))
       if M < 2 or M > n//4: M = max(2, min(160, n//4)) maybe.
       F = build_feature_matrix(n,M)
       # sinusoid inits
       if time.monotonic() < n_deadline:
           inits = make_sinusoid_inits(n, rng, 12)
           for a_init in inits:
              sc = compute_score(a_init)
              if sc < best_score: update
       local_a = None
       local_score = np.inf
       if best_a is not None and best_a.size == n:
          local_a = best_a; local_score=best_score
       elif parent is not None:
          pa = parent if parent.size == n else resample_sequence(parent,n)
          pa = np.clip(pa,0,None); s=pa.sum(); if s>0: pa=pa/s
          local_a=pa; local_score=compute_score(pa)
       restart=0
       while time.monotonic() < n_deadline:
          if local_a is not None and (restart==0 or restart % 3 == 0):
             x0=fit_fourier_x(local_a,M)
             x0=x0 + rng.normal(0.0,0.02,size=2*M)
          elif restart % 2 == 0:
             x0=random_fourier_x(M,rng)
          else:
             sin_inits=make_sinusoid_inits(n,rng,1)
             if len(sin_inits)>0:
                x0=fit_fourier_x(sin_inits[0],M)
             else:
                x0=random_fourier_x(M,rng)
             x0=x0 + rng.normal(0.0,0.05,size=2*M)
          run_budget = min(45.0, (n_deadline - time.monotonic())*0.8)
          if run_budget < 10.0: break
          sc,a = run_param_optimization(n,M,x0,run_budget,time.monotonic(),F)
          if a is not None and sc < best_score:
             best_score=sc; best_a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0).copy()
          if a is not None and sc < local_score:
             local_score=sc; local_a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0).copy()
          restart += 1
          if restart >= 30: break
    return best_score,best_a
```

Now, `active_polish` uses `weighted_grad_a`. Implement `weighted_grad_a`:
```
def weighted_grad_a(a,p,L,n):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); p=np.asarray(p,float)
   ra=a[::-1].copy()
   M=3*n-2; N=_next_power_of_two(M)
   RA=np.fft.rfft(ra,N); P=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
   convres=np.fft.irfft(RA*P,N)[:M]
   g=2.0*convres[n-1:2*n-1]
   return 2.0*n*(g - 2.0*L)
```
Assume sum a1. Use n from a.size. For n=1 okay.

Potential: `active_polish` uses topk near n. If topk=length c, p uniform over all lags, gradient constant? Then no move? For uniform p, ∂weighted c =2 sum a? maybe constant; gx zero. So topk should be less than length. Set topk=min(2*n-1-1, max(1,n//50?)). Use n//100? For n=8000 topk=80. Good. `topk=max(1, min(2*n-2, n//20, 200))`? Use n//50 =160 for 8000. Good. But active peaks maybe fewer. Use topk = min(2*n-5, max(5, n//100))? If n//100=80 for 8000. Use that. Minimum 5. Max 500. So topk=clamp(n//100,5,500). Good.

       if M2 > n2//4: M2=max(2,n2//4)
       F2=build_feature_matrix(n2,M2)
       x2=fit_fourier_x(base_a,M2) + np.random.default_rng(seed+7).normal(0,0.01,size=2*M2)
       t_rem=phase3_deadline-time.monotonic()
       if t_rem > 0:
          sc,a=run_param_optimization(n2,M2,x2,min(240.0,t_rem),time.monotonic(),F2)
          if a is not None and sc < best_score:
             best_score=sc; best_a=np.clip(a,0,1000).copy()
    phase4_deadline=t0+(deadline-t0)*0.97
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < phase4_deadline:
       if best_a.size > 24000:
          refine_a=resample_sequence(best_a,16000)
       else:
          refine_a=best_a.copy()
       t_rem=phase4_deadline-time.monotonic()
       if t_rem > 0:
          sc,a=logit_adam_lse(refine_a,[500.0,3000.0,15000.0,100000.0],t_rem,time.monotonic())
          if a is not None and sc < best_score:
             best_score=sc; best_a=np.clip(a,0,1000).copy()
    # active polish
    if best_a is not None and best_a.size <= 24000 and time.monotonic() < deadline-5:
       t_rem=min(60.0, deadline-time.monotonic()-2)
       if t_rem > 5:
          sc,a=active_polish(best_a,t_rem,time.monotonic())
          if a is not None and sc < best_score:
             best_score=sc; best_a=np.clip(a,0,1000).copy()
    out=finalize_output(best_a,parent,deadline)
    print(f"FINAL len={len(out)}")
    return out
```

```python
"""
Minimize 2*n*max(self-conv)/(sum)^2 over non-negative sequences.

Strategy:
- Exploit scale invariance: keep sequences normalized.
- Use a low-dimensional positive Fourier/soft-max parameterization for broad
  global search over smooth densities.
- Refine in full log-softmax coordinates with LSE-continuation Adam.
- Finish with a small exact active-peak line search and verified output.
"""

import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time
import math

PG = {
    "F": None,
    "beta": 100.0,
    "cache_x": None,
    "cache_score": None,
    "cache_gx": None,
}


def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        seq = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        return None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if p.size == 0:
        return None
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return p / s


def _next_power_of_two(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def fast_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n < 64:
        return np.convolve(a, a)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = _next_power_of_two(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, nfft)[:m]


def compute_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    c = fast_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def lse_score_grad_arr(a, beta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = fast_conv(a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.inf, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = max(1.0, float(beta))
    scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
    exps = np.exp(scaled)
    exp_sum = float(np.sum(exps))

    if exp_sum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(exp_sum):
        p = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        L = cmax
    else:
        p = exps / exp_sum
        L = cmax + float(np.log(exp_sum)) / beta

    ra = a[::-1].copy()
    m2 = 3 * n - 2
    nfft2 = _next_power_of_two(m2)
    RA = np.fft.rfft(ra, nfft2)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, nfft2)
    convres = np.fft.irfft(RA * P, nfft2)[:m2]
    g = 2.0 * convres[n - 1:2 * n - 1]

    s2 = s * s
    s3 = s2 * s
    grad = 2.0 * float(n) * (g / s2 - 2.0 * L / s3)
    score = float(2.0 * n * L / s2)
    return score, grad


def feature_scale(m):
    if m <= 2:
        return 1.0
    return 1.0 / math.sqrt(float(m))


def build_feature_matrix(n, m_freq):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m_freq = int(m_freq)
    if m_freq <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return np.zeros((0, n), dtype=np.float64)
    rows = []
    for m in range(1, m_freq + 1):
        w = 2.0 * math.pi * float(m) / float(n)
        sc = feature_scale(m)
        rows.append(np.cos(w * i) * sc)
        rows.append(np.sin(w * i) * sc)
    return np.vstack(rows).astype(np.float64)


def fit_fourier_x(a, m_freq):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m_freq = int(m_freq)
    if m_freq <= 0 or n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.log(np.clip(a, 1e-30, 1e30))
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    Z = np.fft.rfft(z)
    x = np.zeros(2 * m_freq, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, m_freq + 1):
        if m >= Z.size:
            break
        sc = feature_scale(m)
        c = 2.0 * float(Z[m].real) / float(n)
        sn = -2.0 * float(Z[m].imag) / float(n)
        x[2 * (m - 1)] = c / sc
        x[2 * (m - 1) + 1] = sn / sc
    return x


def random_fourier_x(m_freq, rng):
    return rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=2 * int(m_freq)).astype(np.float64)


def make_sinusoid_inits(n, rng, count=12):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    out = []
    if n <= 0:
        return out

    phases = [math.pi / 2.0, 0.0, math.pi, float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))]
    for r in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.95):
        for ph in phases[:3]:
            a = 1.0 + r * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * i / float(n) + ph)
            a = np.maximum(a, 1e-6)
            s = float(a.sum())
            if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
                out.append(a / s)

    extra = max(0, int(count) - len(out))
    for _ in range(extra):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in (1, 2, 3):
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.6)) / float(m)
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
            a += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * m * i / float(n) + ph)
            amp2 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.25)) / float(m)
            ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
            a += amp2 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * m * i / float(n) + ph2)
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-6)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
            out.append(a / s)
    return out


def resample_sequence(a, new_n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, new_n)
    x_old = np.arange(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, float(a.size - 1), int(new_n), dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, new_n)
    return b / s


def softmax_from_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    z = z - zmax
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a = np.full(z.size, 1.0 / float(z.size), dtype=np.float64)
        return a
    return e / s


def stable_softmax(x):
    return softmax_from_z(x)


def pg_set(F, beta):
    PG["F"] = F
    PG["beta"] = float(beta)
    PG["cache_x"] = None
    PG["cache_score"] = None
    PG["cache_gx"] = None


def pg_make_a(x):
    F = PG["F"]
    if F is None:
        return None
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    z = x.dot(F)
    return softmax_from_z(z)


def pg_compute(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if PG["cache_x"] is not None and x.shape == PG["cache_x"].shape:
        if np.array_equal(x, PG["cache_x"]):
            return PG["cache_score"], PG["cache_gx"]

    F = PG["F"]
    beta = PG["beta"]
    if F is None:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(x.size, dtype=np.float64)

    z = x.dot(F)
    a = softmax_from_z(z)
    sc, ga = lse_score_grad_arr(a, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        ga = np.zeros_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
    gx = a * (ga - float(np.dot(a, ga)))
    gx = np.where(np.isfinite(gx), gx, 0.0)

    PG["cache_x"] = x.copy()
    PG["cache_score"] = sc
    PG["cache_gx"] = gx
    return sc, gx


def pg_func(x):
    sc, _ = pg_compute(x)
    return sc


def pg_grad(x):
    _, gx = pg_compute(x)
    return gx


def run_param_optimization(n, m_freq, x0, time_budget, t_start, F=None):
    if time_budget <= 0.0 or n <= 0:
        return None, None

    if F is None:
        F = build_feature_matrix(n, m_freq)
    V = F.shape[0]
    if V == 0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        return compute_score(a), a

    x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if x.size < V:
        x = np.concatenate([x, np.zeros(V - x.size, dtype=np.float64)])
    elif x.size > V:
        x = x[:V]
    x = np.clip(x, -3.0, 3.0)

    beta_schedule = [20.0, 100.0, 600.0, 3000.0, 12000.0]
    nb = len(beta_schedule)
    if nb == 1:
        fracs = [1.0]
    else:
        final = 0.40
        base = (1.0 - final) / float(nb - 1)
        fracs = [base] * (nb - 1) + [final]

    bounds = [(-3.0, 3.0)] * V
    best_score = np.inf
    best_a = None
    cum = 0.0

    for idx, beta in enumerate(beta_schedule):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= t_start + time_budget:
            break
        cum += fracs[idx]
        beta_deadline = t_start + time_budget * min(1.0, cum)
        time_per_beta = max(0.02, beta_deadline - now)

        pg_set(F, beta)
        try:
            t_est = time.monotonic()
            pg_compute(x)
            per = max(1e-4, time.monotonic() - t_est)
            maxiter = int(np.clip(0.7 * time_per_beta / per, 10.0, 200.0))
        except Exception:
            maxiter = 50

        try:
            res = minimize(
                pg_func,
                x,
                jac=pg_grad,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": max(10, maxiter),
                    "ftol": 1e-11,
                    "gtol": 1e-5,
                    "maxls": 50,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
            xf = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if xf.size == V and np.all(np.isfinite(xf)):
                x = np.clip(xf, -3.0, 3.0)
        except Exception:
            pass

        a = pg_make_a(x)
        if a is not None:
            sc = compute_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()

    if best_a is None:
        a = pg_make_a(x)
        if a is None:
            a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        best_score = compute_score(a)
        best_a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()
    return best_score, best_a


def param_global_search(parent, best_score, best_a, t_start, deadline, seed=12345):
    if deadline <= t_start:
        return best_score, best_a

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    order = []
    if parent is not None:
        pn = int(parent.size)
        if 256 <= pn <= 30000:
            order.append(pn)
    for n in (8192, 16384, 4096):
        if n not in order:
            order.append(n)
        if len(order) >= 4:
            break

    total = deadline - t_start
    if total <= 0.0:
        return best_score, best_a

    weights = []
    for idx in range(len(order)):
        if idx == 0:
            weights.append(0.40)
        elif idx == 1:
            weights.append(0.25)
        else:
            weights.append(0.15)
    wsum = float(sum(weights))
    weights = [w / wsum for w in weights]

    cum = 0.0
    for idx, n in enumerate(order):
        cum += weights[idx]
        n_deadline = min(deadline, t_start + total * min(1.0, cum))
        if n < 256:
            continue

        m_freq = max(20, min(160, n // 50))
        if m_freq > n // 4:
            m_freq = max(2, n // 4)

        F = build_feature_matrix(n, m_freq)

        if time.monotonic() < n_deadline:
            inits = make_sinusoid_inits(n, rng, 12)
            for a_in in inits:
                sc = compute_score(a_in)
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = np.clip(a_in, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()

        local_a = None
        local_score = np.inf
        if best_a is not None and best_a.size == n:
            local_a = best_a
            local_score = best_score
        elif parent is not None:
            pa = parent if parent.size == n else resample_sequence(parent, n)
            pa = np.clip(pa, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s = float(pa.sum())
            if s > 0.0:
                pa = pa / s
            local_a = pa
            local_score = compute_score(pa)

        restart = 0
        while time.monotonic() < n_deadline:
            if local_a is not None and (restart == 0 or restart % 3 == 0):
                x0 = fit_fourier_x(local_a, m_freq)
                x0 += rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, size=2 * m_freq)
            elif restart % 2 == 0:
                x0 = random_fourier_x(m_freq, rng)
            else:
                sin_inits = make_sinusoid_inits(n, rng, 1)
                if len(sin_inits) > 0:
                    x0 = fit_fourier_x(sin_inits[0], m_freq)
                else:
                    x0 = random_fourier_x(m_freq, rng)
                x0 += rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=2 * m_freq)

            run_budget = min(45.0, (n_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.8)
            if run_budget < 10.0:
                break

            sc, a = run_param_optimization(n, m_freq, x0, run_budget, time.monotonic(), F)
            if a is not None and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()
            if a is not None and sc < local_score:
                local_score = sc
                local_a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()

            restart += 1
            if restart >= 30:
                break

    return best_score, best_a


def logit_lse_score_grad(x, beta):
    a = stable_softmax(x)
    sc, ga = lse_score_grad_arr(a, beta)
    gx = a * (ga - float(np.dot(a, ga)))
    gx = np.where(np.isfinite(gx), gx, 0.0)
    return sc, gx, a


def logit_adam_lse(a_init, betas, time_budget, t_start):
    a_init = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64)
    if a_init.size == 0:
        return np.inf, None
    n = a_init.size
    x = np.log(np.clip(a_init, 1e-12, 1e12))
    x = x - float(np.mean(x))

    best_a = np.clip(a_init, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()
    best_score = compute_score(best_a)
    if time_budget <= 0.0 or len(betas) == 0:
        return best_score, best_a

    if len(betas) == 1:
        fracs = [1.0]
    else:
        final = 0.45
        base = (1.0 - final) / float(len(betas) - 1)
        fracs = [base] * (len(betas) - 1) + [final]

    b1 = 0.9
    b2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8
    check_interval = max(20, min(300, n // 40))
    cum = 0.0

    for beta in betas:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= t_start + time_budget:
            break
        cum += fracs[-1] if len(fracs) == 1 else fracs[int(beta)]
        beta_deadline = t_start + time_budget * min(1.0, cum)

        m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        t_beta = 0
        fails = 0

        lr0 = 0.5 / (1.0 + float(beta) / 1000.0)
        if lr0 > 0.5:
            lr0 = 0.5
        if lr0 < 0.003:
            lr0 = 0.003

        while time.monotonic() < beta_deadline:
            sc, gx, a = logit_lse_score_grad(x, beta)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                fails += 1
                x = np.clip(x, -40.0, 40.0)
                x = x - float(np.mean(x))
                if fails > 5:
                    break
                continue
            fails = 0

            m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * gx
            v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (gx * gx)
            t_beta += 1
            m_hat = m / (1.0 - b1 ** t_beta)
            v_hat = v / (1.0 - b2 ** t_beta)
            lr_t = lr0 / (1.0 + 0.0005 * float(t_beta))
            x = x - lr_t * m_hat / (np.sqrt(v_hat) + eps)
            x = x - float(np.mean(x))
            x = np.clip(x, -50.0, 50.0)

            if t_beta % check_interval == 0:
                a_now = stable_softmax(x)
                sc_now = compute_score(a_now)
                if np.isfinite(sc_now) and sc_now < best_score:
                    best_score = sc_now
                    best_a = np.clip(a_now, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()

        a_now = stable_softmax(x)
        sc_now = compute_score(a_now)
        if np.isfinite(sc_now) and sc_now < best_score:
            best_score = sc_now
            best_a = np.clip(a_now, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()

    return best_score, best_a


def weighted_grad_a(a, p, L, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    ra = a[::-1].copy()
    m2 = 3 * n - 2
    nfft2 = _next_power_of_two(m2)
    RA = np.fft.rfft(ra, nfft2)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, nfft2)
    convres = np.fft.irfft(RA * P, nfft2)[:m2]
    g = 2.0 * convres[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    return 2.0 * float(n) * (g - 2.0 * L)


def active_polish(a_init, time_budget, t_start):
    if time_budget <= 0.0 or a_init is None:
        if a_init is None:
            return np.inf, None
        s = compute_score(a_init)
        return s, np.clip(a_init, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()

    a = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, None

    x = np.log(np.clip(a, 1e-12, 1e12))
    x = x - float(np.mean(x))
    a = stable_softmax(x)
    best_score = compute_score(a)
    best_a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()

    topk = n // 100
    if topk < 5:
        topk = 5
    if topk > 500:
        topk = 500
    topk = int(max(1, min(2 * n - 2, topk)))

    deadline = t_start + time_budget
    step = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = fast_conv(a)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        if not np.isfinite(c).all():
            break

        idx = np.argpartition(-c, topk)[:topk]
        p = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        p[idx] = 1.0 / float(topk)
        L = float(np.dot(p, c))

        ga = weighted_grad_a(a, p, L, n)
        gx = a * (ga - float(np.dot(a, ga)))
        gx = np.where(np.isfinite(gx), gx, 0.0)
        nrm = float(np.linalg.norm(gx))
        if nrm <= 1e-14:
            break
        direction = -gx / nrm

        improved = False
        for eps in (0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002):
            x2 = x + eps * direction
            x2 = x2 - float(np.mean(x2))
            x2 = np.clip(x2, -50.0, 50.0)
            a2 = stable_softmax(x2)
            sc2 = compute_score(a2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                x = x2
                a = a2
                best_score = sc2
                best_a = np.clip(a2, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()
                improved = True
                break

        step += 1
        if not improved and step > 4:
            break

    return best_score, best_a


def finalize_output(best_a, parent, deadline):
    arr = None
    if best_a is not None:
        arr = best_a
    elif parent is not None:
        arr = parent

    if arr is None:
        arr = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)

    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        arr = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
        s = 64.0

    # Normalize to a safe sum; score is scale invariant.
    arr = arr / (s / arr.size)

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in arr]
    total = float(sum(out))
    if total < 0.01:
        out = [1.0 / 64.0] * 64

    # Validate if affordable.
    if len(out) <= 20000 and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        try:
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
            if not np.isfinite(val):
                val = compute_score(out)
        except Exception:
            val = compute_score(out)

    final_score = compute_score(out)
    print(
        "finalize: length=%d internal=%.8f" % (len(out), final_score),
        flush=True,
    )
    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    reserve = max(30.0, 0.02 * budget)
    deadline = t_start + budget - reserve

    parent = get_parent_sequence()
    best_score = np.inf
    best_a = None

    if parent is not None:
        best_score = compute_score(parent)
        best_a = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()
        print("parent: n=%d score=%.8f" % (best_a.size, best_score), flush=True)

    phase1_deadline = t_start + (deadline - t_start) * 0.35
    best_score, best_a = param_global_search(
        parent, best_score, best_a, t_start, phase1_deadline, int(seed) + 1
    )
    if best_a is not None:
        print("after param search: n=%d score=%.8f" % (best_a.size, best_score), flush=True)

    phase2_deadline = t_start + (deadline - t_start) * 0.75
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < phase2_deadline:
        if best_a.size > 24000:
            refine_a = resample_sequence(best_a, 16000)
        else:
            refine_a = best_a.copy()
        remaining = phase2_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining > 0.0:
            sc, a = logit_adam_lse(
                refine_a,
                [20.0, 80.0, 400.0, 2000.0, 10000.0, 60000.0],
                remaining,
                time.monotonic(),
            )
            if a is not None and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()
                print(
                    "after logit Adam 1: n=%d score=%.8f" % (best_a.size, best_score),
                    flush=True,
                )

    phase3_deadline = t_start + (deadline - t_start) * 0.91
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < phase3_deadline:
        n2 = int(best_a.size)
        if n2 > 30000:
            n2 = 16000
            base_a = resample_sequence(best_a, n2)
        else:
            base_a = best_a.copy()

        m2 = max(30, min(180, n2 // 40))
        if m2 > n2 // 4:
            m2 = max(2, n2 // 4)

        rng3 = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 7)
        F2 = build_feature_matrix(n2, m2)
        x2 = fit_fourier_x(base_a, m2) + rng3.normal(0.0, 0.01, size=2 * m2)
        remaining = phase3_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining > 0.0:
            sc, a = run_param_optimization(
                n2, m2, x2, min(240.0, remaining), time.monotonic(), F2
            )
            if a is not None and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()
                print(
                    "after second param pass: n=%d score=%.8f" % (best_a.size, best_score),
                    flush=True,
                )

    phase4_deadline = t_start + (deadline - t_start) * 0.97
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < phase4_deadline:
        if best_a.size > 24000:
            refine_a = resample_sequence(best_a, 16000)
        else:
            refine_a = best_a.copy()
        remaining = phase4_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining > 0.0:
            sc, a = logit_adam_lse(
                refine_a,
                [500.0, 3000.0, 15000.0, 100000.0],
                remaining,
                time.monotonic(),
            )
            if a is not None and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()
                print(
                    "after logit Adam 2: n=%d score=%.8f" % (best_a.size, best_score),
                    flush=True,
                )

    if best_a is not None and best_a.size <= 24000 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
        remaining = min(60.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0)
        if remaining > 5.0:
            sc, a = active_polish(best_a, remaining, time.monotonic())
            if a is not None and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0).copy()
                print(
                    "after active polish: n=%d score=%.8f" % (best_a.size, best_score),
                    flush=True,
                )

    return finalize_output(best_a, parent, min(deadline, t_start + budget - 2.0))
```
